```
   if fb is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:L]
   return c, fa
```

LP direction method:
Given current a (sum S0). Compute M = max C. Choose active lags K = indices where C >= M - δ (δ maybe M*0.01 or fixed). Let A be matrix for active rows: row k: (f*g)_k = ∑_{j} f_{k-j} g_j (if 0≤k-j<n). We want find g≥0 satisfying A g ≤ M (or M * scale) and maximize sum g. f has sum S0. We can scale M maybe. Since f feasible sum S0, optimum sum ≥S0. Let gopt. Then normalize g to sum S0: g = gopt * (S0/sum gopt). Update a_new = (1-α) a + α g. The new score exact; accept if improves. This is exactly previous parent step. We can do this a few times.

Solve LP: variables n (6962), constraints K maybe up to few hundred or few thousand, one implicit objective.
```
rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]

    q = k - np.arange(jmin,jmax+1)
    rows.extend(np.full(len(q), k)); cols.extend(range(jmin,jmax+1)); vals.extend(f[q])
A = csr_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)), shape=(K,n), dtype=float64)
```

Update:
```

a = clip; normalize
```

```

```

```
try: from scipy import fft as spfft? use numpy.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
  t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)-20.0
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

  parent_arr=None; parent_score=np.inf; parent_list=None
  try:
     p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
     if p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
        # exact evaluate original
        plist=[float(x) for x in p]
        ps=evaluate_sequence(plist)
        if np.isfinite(ps): parent_arr=p; parent_score=ps
     except: pass
  best_exact_arr=parent_arr.copy() if parent_arr is not None else None
  best_exact_score=parent_score if finite else inf

  # choose working n
  if parent_arr is not None:
      m_old=parent_arr.size
      n = 10000 if m_old < 12000 else (m_old if m_old <=11000 else 10000?) # Should we if parent bigger than 10000? parent is 6962.
      # But if m_old>10000, downsample to 10000? maybe if 12000, 10000.
  else:
      n=8192
```

Function `step(a, fa, C, Cmax, temp, lr, beta1, beta2, step_count, m1, m2)`:
```
shift = temp*(C - Cmax)
# to avoid underflow warnings, np.exp no warning? underflow silent.
w = np.exp(shift)
wsum = float(w.sum())
if wsum <= 0 or not finite:
   w.fill(0); w[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0; wsum=1
else: w /= wsum
M = Cmax + math.log(wsum)/temp
wfl = w[::-1] # maybe length L
fw = np.fft.rfft(wfl, sizeM)
hc = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, sizeM)
H = hc[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
S = float(a.sum())
invS2 = 1/(S*S)
grad = (2.0*n)*invS2*(2.0*H) - (4.0*n*M)*invS2/S
grad -= grad.mean()
# if not finite, return ...
step_count +=1
m1 = b1*m1 + (1-b1)*grad
m2 = b2*m2 + (1-b2)*grad*grad
mh = m1/(1-b1**step_count)
vh = m2/(1-b2**step_count)
update = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
avg = S/n
update = np.clip(update, -0.5*avg, 0.5*avg)
new = a - update
np.clip(new, 0, 1000, out=new)
ss = new.sum()
if ss <= tiny:
   new = a
else: new *= S/ss
return new, step_count, m1,m2
```
Note: `w` length L; rfft with default n=sizeM will pad/truncate to sizeM? If wfl length L < sizeM, rfft(wfl, sizeM) pads zeros. Good. `np.exp(shift)` for shift with C-Cmax maybe up to 0, negative. If temp large e.g. 1000, diff -1e-15 for ties, okay. If C has NaN? C finite.

One issue: `M = Cmax + log(wsum)/temp`. If wsum very large? Since shift max0, each w≤1, wsum≤L, log≤log(20000)=10. If temp small, M up to Cmax+10/temp. For temp3, +3.3, not big. For temp0.1, +100, but temp not too small. Good.

Seed C score:
```
def fast_score(a):
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, sizeM)
   C=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, sizeM)[:L]
   C = np.maximum(C,0,out=C)
   S=a.sum()
   return 2.0*n*C.max()/(S*S)
```
This uses sizeM which >= 2n-1. For seed scoring, okay.

Pseudo optimizer:
```
def optimize(a, budget_until, start_temp..., sections...):
   S_target=float(n)
   a=normalize_to_n(a)
   best_sur=a.copy(); best_sur_sc=fast_score(a)
   m1=np.zeros(n); m2=np.zeros(n)
   corr1=1.0; corr2=1.0; it=0
   b1=0.9; b2=0.99
   eps=1e-8
   now=time.monotonic()
   for ph in sections:
      frac_end, t0,t1,l0,l1 = ...
      phase_end = min(start + frac, limit)
      if phase_end <= now: start=now; continue
      dur=phase_end-now
      while now < phase_end - 0.5 and now < hard_limit:
         # maybe if time < 3s break
         fa = np.fft.rfft(a, sizeM)
         C = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, sizeM)[:L]
         np.maximum(C,0,C,out=C)? np.maximum(C,0,C) works with out? `np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)` yes.
         Cmax = float(C.max())
         S = float(a.sum()) # maybe n
         sc = 2.0*n*Cmax/(S*S)
         if sc < best_sur_sc:
             best_sur_sc=sc; best_sur_arr=a.copy()
         # temp/lr
         alpha = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic()-phase_start)/dur))
         temp=temp0 + (temp1-temp0)*alpha
         lr=lr0 + (lr1-lr0)*alpha
         if temp < 1e-3: temp=1e-3
         # weights
         shift = temp*(C - Cmax)
         # If shift min very negative, exp underflow. Okay. Use np.exp(shift) with underflow warning? Numpy may issue RuntimeWarning? Underflow usually not warning by default? It can. We can suppress? Not necessary but maybe output? We can use `with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore')`.
         with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore'):
             w = np.exp(shift)
         wsum = float(w.sum())
         if not finite or <=0: w.fill(0); w[argmax(C)]=1; wsum=1
         else: w /= wsum
         M = Cmax + math.log(wsum)/temp
         wfl = w[::-1]
         fw = np.fft.rfft(wfl, sizeM)
         hc = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, sizeM)
         H = hc[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
         invS2=1/(S*S)
         grad = (4.0*n/M? no formula) ...
```
Need formula: grad = (2n/S^2)*(2H) - (4nM/S^3). This is `(4.0*n/S**2)*H - (4.0*n*M/S**3)`. Since S~n, first ~4/n*H. Good. Earlier I wrote 2n*invS2*dM with dM=2H ->4nH/S^2.
```
         grad = (4.0 * n * H) * invS2 - (4.0 * n * M) * invS2 / S
         # center
         grad -= grad.mean()
         # if nonfinite, break
         if not all finite: a=best_sur; break
         # Adam
         it +=1
         corr1 *= b1; corr2 *= b2
         m1 = b1*m1 + (1-b1)*grad
         m2 = b2*m2 + (1-b2)*grad*grad
         mh = m1/(1-corr1)
         vh = m2/(1-corr2)
         upd = lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
         # cap
         avg = S/n
         cap = 0.4 * avg  # 40% of mean coordinate
         upd = np.clip(upd, -cap, cap)
         na = a - upd
         np.clip(na, 0.0, 1000.0, out=na)
         ss = float(na.sum())
         if ss <= 1e-12:
             # perturb
             na = a + 1e-6*rng.standard_normal(n)
             np.clip(na,0,1000,out=na); ss=na.sum(); if ss<=0: break
         na *= S/ss
         a = na
         now = time.monotonic()
      # phase end exact? maybe
   return a, best_sur_arr, best_sur_sc
```

For speed, optimize normalize:
```
def normalize_to_n(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        a.fill(1.0); return a
    a *= n / s
    # fix possible cap violations
    bad = a > 1000.0
    if np.any(bad):
       a[bad]=1000.0
    s2=float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 0: a.fill(1.0); return a
    if s2 != n and abs(s2-n)>1e-8*n:
       # add/remove proportionally, without exceeding cap
       diff=n-s2
       if diff>0:
          # add to entries <1000
          capmask=a<1000
          if capmask.sum()>0: a[capmask]+= diff/capmask.sum()
          else: a.fill(1.0)
       else:
          a *= n/s2
          np.clip(a,0,1000,out=a)
    return a
```
This should normalize close. But exact sum may not be exactly n; objective score uses actual S in fast_score/gray. That's fine. We don't need exact S=n, but score formula uses S. For gradient formula we use actual S. Normalization approximate. Good.

Let's define fast_score carefully:
```
def fast_score(a):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, sizeM)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, sizeM)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-12: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n * c.max() / (s*s))
```
This computes full conv via length sizeM; sizeM>=2n-1. Good. For fast seed scoring, no clipping of a? We normalize clips.

```
def maybe_exact(arr, force=False):
   nonlocal best_exact_arr, best_exact_score, exact_count
   if arr is None or exact_count>=12: return
   now=time.monotonic()
   if not force and now > opt_limit - 5: return
   try:
      arr=np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
      if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
      arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
      if float(np.sum(arr)) <0.01: return
      lst=arr.tolist()
      sc=evaluate_sequence(lst)
      exact_count +=1
      if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
          best_exact_score=float(sc); best_exact_arr=arr.copy()
   except Exception: pass
```

Seed generation code:
```
def make_seeds():
  candidates=[]
  def add(a):
    try:
      a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
      if a.size != n:
         if a.size>0:
           # interpolate to n
           x=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=np.interp(x, np.linspace(0,1,a.size), a)
           a=y
      a=normalize_to_n(a)
      if float(a.sum())>1e-9:
         sc=fast_score(a)
         if np.isfinite(sc): candidates.append((sc,a.copy()))
    except: pass
  if parent_arr is not None:
      base=parent_arr
      if n == base.size: i0=base.copy(); i1=base[::-1].copy()
      else:
         x_old=np.linspace(0,1,base.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
         i0=np.interp(x_new,x_old,base)
         i1=np.interp(x_new,x_old,base[::-1])
      add(i0); add(i1)
  # analytic
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  for tilt in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0]:
     add(1.0 + tilt*(0.5 - x))
     add(1.0 - tilt*(0.5 - x))
  for lam in [1,3,6]:
     add(np.exp(lam*(x-0.5)))
     add(np.exp(-lam*(x-0.5)))
  for p,q in [(0,2),(2,0),(0,4),(4,0),(1,3),(3,1)]:
     xx=np.maximum(x,1e-6); xx1=np.maximum(1-x,1e-6)
     add(np.power(xx,p)*np.power(xx1,q) + 1e-6)
  # random smooth
  for sd in [0.1,0.3]:
     a = 1.0 + sd*np.random? Use rng generated low-frequency noise: noise = np.sin(x*2π* (rng.integers(1,5)) + phase) etc.
  # uniform
  add(np.ones(n))
```

Implementation of normalize_to_n:
```
def normalize_to_n(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        if a.size == 0:
            a = np.ones(n)
        elif a.size == 1:
            a = np.full(n, float(a[0]))
        else:
            xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
            xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            a = np.interp(xn, xo, a)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a.fill(1.0)
        return a
    a *= float(n) / s
    # cap and repair
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-12:
        a.fill(1.0)
    else:
        diff = float(n) - s2
        if abs(diff) > 1e-7 * n:
            if diff > 0:
                mask = a < 1000.0
                if mask.any():
                    a[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
                    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
                else:
                    a.fill(1.0)
            else:
                a *= float(n) / s2
                np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    return a
```
If after adding diff to masked, some exceed1000, clip reduces sum; but diff small. Could loop but not needed. For optimizer, use faster `fast_normalize` without mask? The update step uses floor and no cap likely. Use simple:
```
def rescale_to_n(a, S_target=float(n)):
   ss=a.sum(); if ss<=... return ...; a *= S_target/ss; np.clip; ss...; floor...
```
But normalize_to_n robust enough but calls interp if size mismatch; in optimizer a is size n; np.asarray no copy? It may copy if not float. Use in optimizer too? It does interp check and mask, overhead per iteration if called. For optimizer, implement separate `normalize_fast(a)`:
```
def normalize_fast(a, S_target=None):
    S_target = float(n) if S_target is None else float(S_target)
    np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0,out=a)
    # floor
    floor = 1e-9 * S_target / n
    if floor>0: np.maximum(a, floor, out=a)
    ss = float(a.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-12:
        a.fill(S_target/n); return a
    a *= S_target/ss
    np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0,out=a)
    return a
```
This maintains sum close; floor added before scale, sum increases by n*floor=1e-9*S, negligible; scaling corrects. If cap clips after scaling, sum may drop by tiny; next iteration okay. Use in optimizer.

`normalize_to_n` for seeds can call normalize_fast after ensuring length, but seeds don't need floor? Use robust. We can define `prepare_array(a)` for seeds: ensure length n, normalize_fast. Use floor too okay.

In seeds, `add` uses `a = np.asarray...; if size != n interp; normalize_fast(a)`.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _p2(x: int) -> int:
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end_abs = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    # Leave room for final validation and a safe return.
    run_deadline = end_abs - 35.0
    if run_deadline < t0 + 20.0:
        run_deadline = end_abs - 2.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # ----- Recover the supplied parent construction, if available. ----------
    parent_arr = None
    parent_score = np.inf
    parent_eval_time = 0.0

    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) >= 0.01:
                if float(budget_s) >= 60.0:
                    try:
                        te = time.monotonic()
                        ps = evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
                        parent_eval_time = time.monotonic() - te
                        if np.isfinite(ps):
                            parent_arr = p.copy()
                            parent_score = float(ps)
                        else:
                            parent_arr = p.copy()
                            parent_score = np.inf
                    except Exception:
                        parent_arr = p.copy()
                        parent_score = np.inf
                else:
                    parent_arr = p.copy()
                    parent_score = np.inf
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None
        parent_score = np.inf

    # If budget is tiny, return the known valid construction immediately.
    if float(budget_s) < 60.0:
        if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size > 0:
            out = np.clip(np.asarray(parent_arr, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(out.sum()) >= 0.01:
                return [float(x) for x in out]
        return [1.0]

    # ----- Select working resolution. ---------------------------------------
    MAX_N = 10000
    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size > 0:
        m = int(parent_arr.size)
        if parent_eval_time > 1e-3:
            # Keep an estimated final exact evaluation within the reserved time.
            scale2 = min(4.0, 12.0 / max(1e-3, float(parent_eval_time)))
            if scale2 < 1.0:
                scale2 = 1.0
            n_est = int(m * math.sqrt(scale2))
        else:
            n_est = int(m * 1.15)

        n = int(min(MAX_N, max(m, n_est)))

        if float(budget_s) < 200.0:
            n = min(n, m)
    else:
        n = 6962

    n = int(max(1, n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    sizeM = _p2(3 * n - 2)
    target_S = float(n)
    T = max(1.0, 0.75 * float(n))
    invT = 1.0 / T

    # ----- Normalization helpers. ------------------------------------------
    def normalize_fast(a: np.ndarray, S_target: float = None) -> np.ndarray:
        if S_target is None:
            S_target = target_S
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)

        # Keep a mild positive floor to avoid pathological full zeros.
        floor = 1e-10 * float(S_target) / float(n)
        if floor > 0.0:
            np.maximum(a, floor, out=a)

        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            a.fill(float(S_target) / float(n))
            return a

        a *= float(S_target) / s
        np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
        return a

    def prepare_length_n(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            if a.size == 0:
                a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            elif a.size == 1:
                a = np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
                xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                a = np.interp(xn, xo, np.array(a, dtype=np.float64))
        return normalize_fast(a)

    # ----- Fast interior scoring using FFT convolution. --------------------
    def fast_score(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
        if a is None:
            return np.inf
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return np.inf
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, sizeM)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, sizeM)[:L]
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return np.inf
        cmx = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmx):
            return np.inf
        return float(2.0 * n * cmx / (s * s))

    # ----- Exact-evaluation wrapper with strict time/count guards. ----------
    best_exact_arr = parent_arr.copy() if parent_arr is not None else None
    best_exact_score = parent_score if np.isfinite(parent_score) else np.inf
    exact_count = 0

    def maybe_exact(arr, force: bool = False):
        nonlocal best_exact_arr, best_exact_score, exact_count
        if arr is None:
            return
        if exact_count >= 10:
            return
        now = time.monotonic()
        if not force and now > run_deadline - 5.0:
            return
        if force and now > end_abs - 10.0:
            return
        try:
            x = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if x.size == 0:
                return
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                return
            s = float(x.sum())
            if s < 0.01:
                return
            exact_count += 1
            sc = evaluate_sequence(x.tolist())
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                sc = float(sc)
                if sc < best_exact_score:
                    best_exact_score = sc
                    best_exact_arr = x.copy()
        except Exception:
            exact_count += 1

    # ----- Generate and rank a few initializations. ------------------------
    seed_list = []

    def add_seed(a):
        try:
            a = prepare_length_n(a)
            sc = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                seed_list.append((sc, a.copy()))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size > 0:
        base = np.asarray(parent_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if n == base.size:
            add_seed(base)
            add_seed(base[::-1].copy())
        else:
            xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, base.size)
            xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            add_seed(np.interp(xn, xo, base))
            add_seed(np.interp(xn, xo, base[::-1].copy()))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    # Smooth monotone and tilted shapes.
    for tilt in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        add_seed(1.0 + tilt * (0.5 - x))
        add_seed(1.0 - tilt * (0.5 - x))

    for lam in (1.0, 3.0, 6.0):
        add_seed(np.exp(lam * (x - 0.5)))
        add_seed(np.exp(-lam * (x - 0.5)))

    xx = np.maximum(x, 1e-6)
    xy = np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-6)
    for p, q in ((0, 2), (2, 0), (0, 4), (4, 0), (1, 3), (3, 1)):
        add_seed(np.power(xx, p) * np.power(xy, q) + 1e-6)

    # Two lightly smooth periodic perturbations.
    for k, ph in ((2, 0.7), (5, 2.3)):
        add_seed(1.0 + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k * x + ph))
        add_seed(1.0 + 0.15 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * x + ph))

    add_seed(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if not seed_list:
        a0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        normalize_fast(a0)
        start_a = a0
        start_score = 2.0
    else:
        seed_list.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
        start_score, start_a = seed_list[0]

    maybe_exact(start_a, force=False)

    # If there is no meaningful time left, return the best exact candidate.
    if time.monotonic() > run_deadline - 10.0:
        if best_exact_arr is not None:
            return [float(v) for v in np.clip(best_exact_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        return [float(v) for v in start_a]

    # ----- Main annealed projected-gradient / Adam search. -----------------
    best_sur_arr = start_a.copy()
    best_sur_score = start_score

    a = start_a.copy()
    m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    corr1 = 1.0
    corr2 = 1.0
    it = 0
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.99
    eps = 1e-8
    last_improve = 0

    # Phases: (fraction_of_run, beta_start, beta_end, lr_start, lr_end)
    sections = (
        (0.14,   3.0,   10.0,  0.013,  0.008),
        (0.20,  10.0,   50.0,  0.008,  0.0045),
        (0.20,  50.0,  300.0,  0.0045, 0.0025),
        (0.24, 300.0, 5000.0,  0.0025, 0.0010),
        (0.16, 5000.0, 20000.0, 0.0010, 0.0004),
        (0.06, 20000.0, 80000.0, 0.0004, 0.00015),
    )

    opt_start = time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic() > run_deadline:
        opt_start = t0

    total_opt_time = max(1.0, run_deadline - opt_start)

    for pidx, (frac, beta0, beta1, lr0, lr1) in enumerate(sections):
        phase_start = time.monotonic()
        phase_end = min(opt_start + (frac * total_opt_time), run_deadline)
        # If an early phase overshot due to timing, use remaining budget.
        used = max(1e-6, sum(s[0] for s in sections[:pidx]) * total_opt_time)
        phase_end = min(opt_start + ((used + frac) * total_opt_time), run_deadline)
        if phase_end <= phase_start:
            continue

        dur = max(1e-6, phase_end - phase_start)

        while time.monotonic() < phase_end - 0.25 and time.monotonic() < run_deadline - 0.5:
            now = time.monotonic()
            frac_phase = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - phase_start) / dur))
            beta = float(beta0 + (beta1 - beta0) * frac_phase)
            lr = float(lr0 + (lr1 - lr0) * frac_phase)
            if beta <= 1e-3:
                beta = 1e-3

            try:
                fa = np.fft.rfft(a, sizeM)
                c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, sizeM)[:L]
                np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

                cmax = float(c.max())
                if not np.isfinite(cmax):
                    a = best_sur_arr.copy()
                    break

                s = float(a.sum())
                if s <= 1e-12:
                    a = best_sur_arr.copy()
                    break

                score = float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))
                if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_sur_score - 1e-12:
                    best_sur_score = score
                    best_sur_arr = a.copy()
                    last_improve = it

                # Soft weights with a dimensionless sharpness beta.
                shift = beta * invT * (c - cmax)
                np.exp(shift, out=shift)
                w = shift
                wsum = float(w.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
                    w.fill(0.0)
                    w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                else:
                    w /= wsum

                # Weighted objective value used in the scale term.
                M = float(np.dot(w, c))
                if not np.isfinite(M):
                    M = cmax

                # Gradient of the weighted self-convolution objective.
                wfl = w[::-1]
                fw = np.fft.rfft(wfl, sizeM)
                hc = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, sizeM)
                H = hc[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1].copy()
                if H.size:
                    np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)

                invS2 = 1.0 / (s * s)
                coef1 = 4.0 * float(n) * invS2
                coef2 = coef1 * M / s
                grad = coef1 * H - coef2
                grad -= grad.mean()

                if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
                    a = best_sur_arr.copy()
                    break

                # Adam update.
                it += 1
                corr1 *= b1
                corr2 *= b2
                m1 = b1 * m1 + (1.0 - b1) * grad
                m2 = b2 * m2 + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)
                mhat = m1 / (1.0 - corr1)
                vhat = m2 / (1.0 - corr2)
                upd = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)

                # Bound the absolute move to keep iterates sensible.
                avg = s / float(n)
                cap = 0.45 * avg
                if cap > 0.0:
                    np.clip(upd, -cap, cap, out=upd)

                na = a - upd
                np.clip(na, 0.0, 1000.0, out=na)

                # Mild positivity floor, then renormalize.
                fl = 1e-10 * target_S / float(n)
                if fl > 0.0:
                    np.maximum(na, fl, out=na)

                ss = float(na.sum())
                if ss <= 1e-12:
                    na = best_sur_arr.copy()
                else:
                    na *= target_S / ss
                    np.clip(na, 0.0, 1000.0, out=na)

                a = na

                # Occasional low-amplitude kick only in early/mid phases if stuck.
                if (pidx < 3 and
                    it - last_improve > 12000 and
                    time.monotonic() < run_deadline - 80.0 and
                    rng.random() < 0.30):
                    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
                    a = a * np.exp(0.02 * noise)
                    a = normalize_fast(a)
                    m1.fill(0.0)
                    m2.fill(0.0)
                    corr1 = 1.0
                    corr2 = 1.0
                    last_improve = it

            except Exception:
                a = best_sur_arr.copy()
                break

        # Periodic exact bookkeeping.
        if exact_count < 8 and time.monotonic() < run_deadline - 15.0:
            maybe_exact(a, force=False)
            if best_sur_arr is not None and best_sur_score < best_exact_score:
                maybe_exact(best_sur_arr, force=False)

    # ----- Final packaged validation. --------------------------------------
    if best_sur_arr is not None and (not np.isfinite(best_exact_score) or
                                     best_sur_score < best_exact_score - 1e-9):
        maybe_exact(best_sur_arr, force=True)

    # If the current iterate was better than the saved surrogate for some reason.
    if a is not None and best_sur_arr is not None:
        if time.monotonic() < end_abs - 15.0:
            scur = fast_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(scur) and scur < best_sur_score - 1e-9:
                maybe_exact(a, force=True)

    # Choose return array.
    if best_exact_arr is not None:
        out_arr = np.asarray(best_exact_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    elif best_sur_arr is not None:
        out_arr = np.asarray(best_sur_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    else:
        out_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if out_arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(out_arr)) or float(out_arr.sum()) < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(max(1, min(n, 1000)), dtype=np.float64)
        out_arr /= out_arr.size
        out_arr *= max(10.0, float(out_arr.size) * 0.1)

    # Last exact check on the exact object being returned, if there is safe time.
    if time.monotonic() < end_abs - 12.0 and exact_count < 12:
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence(out_arr.tolist())
            if np.isfinite(sc) and best_exact_score != sc:
                # Keep the better of the previously chosen best and this final list.
                # This is only for safety; the object returned is fixed below.
                pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Ensure a plain finite Python list.
    out_list = []
    for v in out_arr:
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            out_list.append(0.0)
        else:
            out_list.append(float(v))

    if not out_list:
        out_list = [1.0]

    return out_list
```
